Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any dp module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 688. The projective resolution has length at most 972 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to dp structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 688/972 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the dp framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 31. The resulting operator achieves bound O(938/n^4).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of structural decomposition and the invariant on the space spanned by dp generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 31, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 938. QED.
